<h2 class='date-header'><span>Thursday, 13 December 2018</span></h2>

          <div class="date-posts">
        
<div class='post-outer'>
<div class='post hentry uncustomized-post-template' itemprop='blogPost' itemscope='itemscope' itemtype='http://schema.org/BlogPosting'>
<meta content='4705414451869398457' itemprop='blogId'/>
<meta content='3719971990088403357' itemprop='postId'/>
<a name='3719971990088403357'></a>
<h3 class='post-title entry-title' itemprop='name'>
Church in Swaziland welcoming LGBTIQ people reopens, but no let-up on discrimination in the kingdom 
</h3>
<div class='post-header'>
<div class='post-header-line-1'></div>
</div>
<div class='post-body entry-content' id='post-body-3719971990088403357' itemprop='description articleBody'>
<div class="post-header">
</div>
<div class="MsoNormal">
<span style="font-family: &quot;times new roman&quot; , serif; font-size: 12.0pt; line-height: 107%;">A church in Swaziland / eSwatini that welcomes and
supports LGBTIQ people <span class="MsoHyperlink"><a href="http://new.observer.org.sz/details.php?id=7576">has reopened.</a></span></span><br />
<br /></div>
<div class="MsoNormal">
</div>
<div class="MsoNormal">
<span style="font-family: &quot;times new roman&quot; , serif; font-size: 12.0pt; line-height: 107%;">Homosexual acts are illegal in the kingdom and LGBTIQ
(lesbian, gay, bisexual, transgender, intersex, queer) people face daily
discrimination.</span><br />
<br /></div>
<div class="MsoNormal">
</div>
<div class="MsoNormal">
<span style="font-family: &quot;times new roman&quot; , serif; font-size: 12.0pt; line-height: 107%;">The branch of the Ark of Joy International Ministry was relaunched in
Coates Valley. The <span class="MsoHyperlink"><a href="http://new.observer.org.sz/details.php?id=7576">Sunday edition of the <i style="mso-bidi-font-style: normal;">Swazi Observer </i>reported</a></span> the
church in Swaziland had closed some years ago after &#8216;membership tapered off&#8217;.</span><br />
<br /></div>
<div class="MsoNormal">
</div>
<div class="MsoNormal">
<span style="font-family: &quot;times new roman&quot; , serif; font-size: 12.0pt; line-height: 107%;">The news of the relaunch was welcomed by the Rock of
Hope which campaigns for LGBTI equality in Swaziland. <span class="MsoHyperlink"><a href="http://new.observer.org.sz/details.php?id=7598">Spokesperson<b style="mso-bidi-font-weight: normal;"><i style="mso-bidi-font-style: normal;"> </i></b>Melusi
Simelane said,</a></span> &#8216;It is worth noting that many in the religious
circles, continue to spew hate speech and show utter disregard for the deeds of
the Lord, by being judgmental and expelling some of the LGBTI community from
their places of worship. It is for that reason, we welcome the opening of such
churches as those that show the love of God, and preach the spirit of oneness
and togetherness.&#8217;</span><br />
<br /></div>
<div class="MsoNormal">
</div>
<div class="MsoNormal">
<span style="font-family: &quot;times new roman&quot; , serif; font-size: 12.0pt; line-height: 107%;">Meanwhile, Gender Links, an advocacy group based in
Johannesburg, South Africa, reported LGBTI people in Swaziland </span><span style="font-family: &quot;times new roman&quot; , serif; font-size: 12.0pt; line-height: 107%;">expressed
concern about the lack of respect shown to them because of their sexual
orientation.</span><br />
<br /></div>
<div class="MsoNormal">
</div>
<div class="MsoNormal">
<span class="MsoHyperlink"><span style="font-family: &quot;times new roman&quot; , serif; font-size: 12.0pt; line-height: 107%;"><a href="http://genderlinks.org.za/news/eswatini-violence-against-key-populations-must-end/">Sifiso
Nhlabatsi, writing on the Gender Links website,</a></span></span><span style="font-family: &quot;times new roman&quot; , serif; font-size: 12.0pt; line-height: 107%;"> said LGBTI people had
tried to engage churches </span><span style="font-family: &quot;times new roman&quot; , serif; font-size: 12.0pt; line-height: 107%;">to sensitize them about their rights</span><span style="font-family: &quot;times new roman&quot; , serif; font-size: 12.0pt; line-height: 107%;">
but had little success.</span><br />
<br /></div>
<div class="MsoNormal">
</div>
<div class="MsoNormal">
<span style="font-family: &quot;times new roman&quot; , serif; font-size: 12.0pt; line-height: 107%;">Nhlabatsi</span><span style="font-family: &quot;times new roman&quot; , serif; font-size: 12.0pt; line-height: 107%;"> wrote about a meeting </span><span style="font-family: &quot;times new roman&quot; , serif; font-size: 12.0pt; line-height: 107%;">held
between pastors and members of the LGBTI community in August 2018. &#8216;During the
meeting which was attended by over 20 pastors and I was also part of, pastors
made it clear that they cannot allow gay people to &#8220;flaunt&#8221; their behaviour in
front of congregants. Pastors said what is being done by LGBTI community&nbsp;
is &#8220;demonic&#8221; and through prayer maybe can be healed.&#8217;</span><br />
<br /></div>
<div class="MsoNormal">
</div>
<div class="MsoNormal">
<span style="font-family: &quot;times new roman&quot; , serif; font-size: 12.0pt; line-height: 107%;">Nhlabatsi</span><span style="font-family: &quot;times new roman&quot; , serif; font-size: 12.0pt; line-height: 107%;"> added, &#8216;</span><span style="font-family: &quot;times new roman&quot; , serif; font-size: 12.0pt; line-height: 107%;">The
meeting which started off on a good note ended on a sour note as the two
parties had a clash of opinions. Senior Pastors in the country did not even
bother to attend the dialogue.&#8217;</span><br />
<br /></div>
<div class="MsoNormal">
</div>
<div class="MsoNormal">
<span style="font-family: &quot;times new roman&quot; , serif; font-size: 12.0pt; line-height: 107%;">Swaziland held <a href="https://swazimedia.blogspot.com/2018/07/lgbti-pride-gets-global-attention.html">its
first LGBTI Pride parade</a> on 30 June 2018. </span><span style="font-family: &quot;times new roman&quot; , serif; font-size: 12.0pt; line-height: 107%;">Swaziland police
went on record to state <span class="MsoHyperlink"><a href="https://swazimedia.blogspot.com/2018/06/observer-misreports-police-on-lgbti.html">it
did not support the march</a></span>. Separately, Superintendent Khulani Mamba,
the official spokesperson for the police and a self-proclaimed prophet, <span class="MsoHyperlink"><a href="https://swazimedia.blogspot.com/2018/06/police-spokesperson-condemns-lgbti.html">preached
from the pulpit,</a></span> &#8216;We say no to homosexuality, this country will not
tolerate the LGBTI community.&#8217;&nbsp;</span><br />
<br /></div>
<div class="MsoNormal">
</div>
<div class="MsoNormal">
<span style="font-family: &quot;times new roman&quot; , serif; font-size: 12.0pt; line-height: 107%;">One of the kingdom&#8217;s best-known NGO rights activist
groups the Swaziland Action Group Against Abuse (SWAGAA) <span class="MsoHyperlink"><a href="https://swazimedia.blogspot.com/2018/06/ngo-snubs-lgbti-what-will-funder-say.html">snubbed
the Pride festival</a></span> saying it was against Biblical teaching.&nbsp;</span><br />
<br /></div>
<div class="MsoNormal">
</div>
<div class="MsoNormal">
<span style="font-family: &quot;times new roman&quot; , serif; font-size: 12.0pt; line-height: 107%;">Swaziland is a tiny landlocked kingdom with a
population of about 1.1 million people, mostly living in rural communities. It
is ruled by King Mswati III who is one of the world&#8217;s last absolute monarchs
who reportedly described homosexuality&nbsp;<span class="MsoHyperlink"><a href="https://www.thedailybeast.com/the-lgbt-heroes-fighting-to-hold-the-first-ever-pride-in-swaziland" target="_blank">as being &#8216;satanic&#8217;</a></span>.</span><br />
<br />
<span style="font-family: &quot;times new roman&quot; , serif; font-size: 12.0pt; line-height: 107%;">In the run up to the event, the <i style="mso-bidi-font-style: normal;">Swazi Observer</i>, a newspaper in effect owned by the King, <span class="MsoHyperlink"><a href="https://swazimedia.blogspot.com/2018/06/observer-steps-up-lgbti-hate-campaign.html">ran
three articles</a></span> calling LGBTI people &#8216;a curse&#8217; and &#8216;evil&#8217; and
likening them to child sex molesters and people who had sex with animals.</span></div>
<div class="MsoNormal">
<br /></div>
<div class="MsoNormal">
</div>
<div class="MsoNormal">
<span style="font-family: &quot;times new roman&quot; , serif; font-size: 12.0pt; line-height: 107%;">There is a great deal of prejudice against LGBTI
people in Swaziland. In May 2016, Rock of Hope, which organised the Pride
event, <span class="MsoHyperlink"><a href="http://www.upr-info.org/sites/default/files/.../swaziland/...25.../js4_upr25_swz_e_main.pdf">reported
to the United Nations Universal Periodic Review</a></span> on Swaziland that
laws, social stigma and prejudice prevented LGBTI organisations from operating
freely.</span><br />
<br /></div>
<div class="MsoNormal">
</div>
<div class="MsoNormal">
<span style="font-family: &quot;times new roman&quot; , serif; font-size: 12.0pt; line-height: 107%;">The report, presented jointly with three South
African-based organisations, stated, &#8216;In Swaziland sexual health rights of LGBT[I]
are not protected. There is inequality in the access to general health care,
gender affirming health care as opposed to sex affirming health care and sexual
reproductive health care and rights of these persons. HIV prevention, testing,
treatment and care services continue to be hetero-normative in nature only
providing for specific care for men born as male and women born as female,
thereby leaving out trans men and women as an unprotected population which
continues to render the state&#8217;s efforts at addressing the spread and incidence
of HIV within general society futile.&#8217;</span><br />
<br /></div>
<div class="MsoNormal">
</div>
<div class="MsoNormal">
<span style="font-family: &quot;times new roman&quot; , serif; font-size: 12.0pt; line-height: 107%;">The report added, &#8216;LGBT[I]s are discriminated and
condemned openly by society. This is manifest in negative statements uttered by
influential people in society e.g., religious, traditional and political
leaders. Traditionalists and conservative Christians view LGBT[I]s as against
Swazi tradition and religion. There have been several incidents where
traditionalists and religious leaders have issued negative statements about
lesbians.<span style="mso-spacerun: yes;">&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span><br />
<br /></div>
<div class="MsoNormal">
</div>
<div class="MsoNormal">
<span style="font-family: &quot;times new roman&quot; , serif; font-size: 12.0pt; line-height: 107%;">&#8216;Human rights abuses and violations against members of
the lesbian, gay, bisexual, transgender and intersex population continue to go
undocumented, unreported, unprosecuted and not addressed.&#8217;</span><span style="font-family: &quot;times new roman&quot; , serif; font-size: 12.0pt; line-height: 107%;">&nbsp;</span><br />
<br />
<span style="font-family: &quot;times new roman&quot; , serif; font-size: 12.0pt; line-height: 107%;">It added, &#8216;There is no legislation recognizing LGBT[I]s
or protecting the right to a non-heterosexual orientation and gender identity
and as a result LGBT[I] cannot be open about their orientation or gender
identity for fear of rejection and discrimination. For example, the Marriage
Act, only recognizes a marriage or a union between a man and a woman. Because
of the absence of a law allowing homosexuals to conclude neither marriage nor
civil unions, same-sex partners cannot adopt children in Swaziland.&#8217;</span></div>
<div class="MsoNormal">
<br /></div>
<div class="MsoNormal">
</div>
<div class="MsoNormal">
<span style="font-family: &quot;times new roman&quot; , serif; font-size: 12.0pt; line-height: 107%;">See also</span><br />
<br /></div>
<div class="MsoNormal">
</div>
<div class="MsoNormal" style="margin-bottom: .0001pt; margin-bottom: 0cm;">
<span style="font-family: &quot;times new roman&quot; , serif; font-size: 12.0pt; line-height: 107%;">LGBT Pride film shows what it&#8217;s like to live with
prejudice and ignorance in Swaziland </span></div>
<div class="MsoNormal" style="margin-bottom: .0001pt; margin-bottom: 0cm;">
<span class="MsoHyperlink"><span style="font-family: &quot;times new roman&quot; , serif; font-size: 12.0pt; line-height: 107%;"><a href="https://swazimedia.blogspot.com/2018/11/lgbt-pride-film-shows-what-its-like-to.html">https://swazimedia.blogspot.com/2018/11/lgbt-pride-film-shows-what-its-like-to.html</a></span></span><br />
<br /></div>
<div class="MsoNormal" style="margin-bottom: .0001pt; margin-bottom: 0cm;">
</div>
<div class="MsoNormal" style="margin-bottom: .0001pt; margin-bottom: 0cm;">
<span style="font-family: &quot;times new roman&quot; , serif; font-size: 12.0pt; line-height: 107%;">LGBTI
Pride gets global attention</span></div>
<div class="MsoNormal" style="margin-bottom: .0001pt; margin-bottom: 0cm;">
<span class="MsoHyperlink"><span style="font-family: &quot;times new roman&quot; , serif; font-size: 12.0pt; line-height: 107%;"><a href="https://swazimedia.blogspot.com/2018/07/lgbti-pride-gets-global-attention.html">https://swazimedia.blogspot.com/2018/07/lgbti-pride-gets-global-attention.html</a></span></span><br />
<br /></div>
<div class="MsoNormal" style="margin-bottom: .0001pt; margin-bottom: 0cm;">
</div>
<div class="MsoNormal" style="margin-bottom: .0001pt; margin-bottom: 0cm;">
<span style="font-family: &quot;times new roman&quot; , serif; font-size: 12.0pt; line-height: 107%;">&#8216;Observer&#8217;
steps up LGBTI hate campaign</span></div>
<span class="MsoHyperlink"><span style="font-family: &quot;times new roman&quot; , serif; font-size: 12.0pt; line-height: 107%;"><a href="https://swazimedia.blogspot.com/2018/06/observer-steps-up-lgbti-hate-campaign.html">https://swazimedia.blogspot.com/2018/06/observer-steps-up-lgbti-hate-campaign.html</a></span></span>
